### EAC029

原段落 00:22:27 · 原文块 43 · 原始字符 432–599

原文片段：

```text
Transmitir next to sense.De cham.¿What is you mean to Science the chun?In my Standard Sism I.De Mac.Layer.Ask de Fish, Física Layer.Two performa this Channel Essencin.
```

校订片段：

```text
transmitter needs to sense the channel. What does it mean to sense the channel? In standard CSMA, the MAC layer asks the physical layer to perform this channel sensing.
```

- 把握：medium；语境推定：否

### EBA002

原段落 00:38:51 · 原文块 73 · 原始字符 403–433

原文片段：

```text
That said, distributed scheme.
```

校订片段：

```text
That's a distributed scheme. The second one that we are going to mention later is instead centralized. What does it mean?〔补回 EBA002，待核〕
```

- 理由：模型恢复集中式与分布式过渡，不将下一段集中式解释误接成分布式。
- 把握：medium；语境推定：否
- 不确定性：见理由及正文标注；本条不声称逐词听辨确认。

### EBA004

原段落 00:41:39 · 原文块 81 · 原始字符 685–733

原文片段：

```text
they will align their, their night clock to the.
```

校订片段：

```text
they will align their, their internal clock to the time reference given by the access point. So everyone aligns its own clock to the one of the access point. The access point measures noon, it's noon for everyone, okay.〔补回 EBA004，待核〕
```
